```csharp
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Narration;

/// <summary>
/// Narration by LLM (spec §8.7), on its own budget. The prompt carries the template prose plus the facts, motives,
/// accounts and lore stored on the events; answers are cached in the journal by event, so reading the same
/// passage twice costs nothing. Without an answer, the template narration is returned.
/// </summary>
public sealed class LlmNarrator(StoryContext story) : INarrator
{
    public const int MaxLength = 2000;

    private readonly TemplateNarrator _templates = new(story.World);

    public string Narrate(WorldEvent ev) =>
        Ask($"narrate|event|{ev.Id}", ev.Date.Day, Facts(ev), "Retell this event in two or three sentences.") ?? _templates.Narrate(ev);

    public string NarrateChain(ChainNode chain)
    {
        var facts = new StringBuilder();
        var depth = 0;
        void Walk(ChainNode node, int level)
        {
            depth = Math.Max(depth, level);
            facts.AppendLine($"{new string(' ', level * 2)}{(level == 0 ? "" : "caused by: ")}{Facts(node.Event)}");
            foreach (var cause in node.Causes) Walk(cause, level + 1);
        }
        Walk(chain, 0);
        return Ask($"narrate|chain|{chain.Event.Id}|{depth}", chain.Event.Date.Day, facts.ToString(),
                   "Tell how this came to pass, from the earliest cause to the event itself, in one paragraph of at most six sentences.")
               ?? _templates.NarrateChain(chain);
    }

    private string Facts(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var line = new StringBuilder(PromptText.Data(_templates.Narrate(ev), 600));
        if (ev.Payload.TryGetValue("lore", out var lore)) line.Append($" [lore: {PromptText.Data(lore, 400)}]");
        return line.ToString();
    }

    private string? Ask(string key, long day, string facts, string task)
    {
        if (!story.Gateway.MayAnswer(ModelTier.Narrate, key)) return null;
        var prompt = PromptText.Section("brief", facts) + $"\n\n{task} Write as the world's chronicler; use only the facts given.";
        var schema = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
        {
            type = "object",
            properties = new { text = new { type = "string" } },
            required = new[] { "text" },
            additionalProperties = false,
        });
        var request = new LlmRequest(ModelTier.Narrate, key, story.Bible, prompt, schema, MaxTokens: 1500);
        var json = story.Gateway.Ask(request, day, answer => Text(answer) is not null);
        return json is null ? null : Text(json);
    }

    private static string? Text(string json)
    {
        try
        {
            using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
            if (doc.RootElement.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Object || !doc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("text", out var t)
                || t.ValueKind != JsonValueKind.String) return null;
            var text = t.GetString()!.Trim();
            return text.Length is > 0 and <= MaxLength ? text : null;
        }
        catch (JsonException)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Narration/TemplateNarrator.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Narration;

/// <summary>Turns events into prose (spec §8.7).</summary>
public interface INarrator
{
    /// <summary>One event as a sentence or two.</summary>
    string Narrate(WorldEvent ev);

    /// <summary>A why-chain as a short passage: the event, then what led to it.</summary>
    string NarrateChain(ChainNode chain);
}

/// <summary>
/// Per-event-type templates with connectives ("because", "which followed") — the offline narrator and the test
/// baseline. Placeholders: <c>{role}</c> names an actor, <c>{@place}</c> the place, <c>{=key}</c> a payload value.
/// A sentence whose placeholder is missing falls back to a generic line, so new event types always read.
/// </summary>
public sealed partial class TemplateNarrator(World world) : INarrator
{
    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> Templates = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
    {
        ["AllianceBroken"] = "The alliance between {a} and {b} was broken.",
        ["AllianceFormed"] = "{a} and {b} swore an alliance.",
        ["AllyJoinedWar"] = "{ally} took up arms in {war}.",
        ["ArmyRaised"] = "{realm} raised {army}, {=soldiers} strong, for {war}.",
        ["ArmyRouted"] = "{army} of {realm} broke and fled at {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactClaimed"] = "{claimant} claimed {artifact} from the treasury of {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactCurseRevealed"] = "The curse of {artifact} showed itself in {holder}: {=curse}.",
        ["ArtifactForged"] = "{maker} forged {artifact} at {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactFound"] = "{delver} found {artifact} in the depths of {@place}.",
        ["ArtifactInherited"] = "{heir} inherited {artifact}.",
        ["ArtifactStolen"] = "{thief} stole {artifact} from {victim}.",
        ["BattleFought"] = "At {@place}, {victor} defeated {vanquished} in a battle of {war}.",
        ["CapitalMoved"] = "{realm} moved its seat to {@place}.",
        ["CharacterBorn"] = "{child} was born at {@place} to {mother} and {father}.",
        ["CharacterDied"] = "{deceased} died of {=cause} at {=age}.",
        ["ComingOfAge"] = "{character} came of age, dreaming of {=ambition}.",
        ["CouncilAppointed"] = "{member} became {=seat} of {realm}.",
        ["DelveFailed"] = "{delver} sought {artifact} in {@place} and failed.",
        ["DuelFought"] = "{challenger} fought {defender} {=stakes} at {@place}.",
        ["EraBegan"] = "{=era} began.",
        ["FoodShortageBegan"] = "Food ran short in {@place}.",
        ["FriendshipFormed"] = "{a} and {b} became friends.",
        ["HolyWarProclaimed"] = "{a} proclaimed a holy war against {b} in the name of {=faith}.",
        ["IndependenceDeclared"] = "{realm} threw off the rule of {former liege}.",
        ["MarriageWed"] = "{spouse} and {partner} were wed at {@place}.",
        ["MonsterHuntFailed"] = "{hunter} hunted the monsters of {@place} and was driven back.",
        ["MonstersSlain"] = "{hunter} slew the monsters of {@place}.",
        ["NewLordRose"] = "{lord} rose to rule {@place}.",
        ["PeaceSigned"] = "{war} ended: {victor} over {vanquished} ({=terms}).",
        ["PlagueOutbreak"] = "Plague broke out in {@place}.",
        ["RealmFell"] = "{realm} fell.",
        ["RegionCeded"] = "{@place} passed from {from} to {to}.",
        ["RegionLiberated"] = "{realm} freed {@place}.",
        ["RegionOccupied"] = "{army} of {realm} took {@place}.",
        ["RevoltBegan"] = "{@place} rose in revolt.",
        ["RivalryBegan"] = "{a} and {b} became rivals over {=over}.",
        ["RivalryDeclared"] = "{a} and {b} declared each other rivals.",
        ["SettlementAbandoned"] = "{@place} was abandoned.",
        ["SettlementSacked"] = "{commander} sacked {@place}; {=dead} died.",
        ["SiegeBegan"] = "{army} of {realm} laid siege to {@place}.",
        ["SiegeEnded"] = "The siege of {@place} ended: it {=outcome}.",
        ["SuccessionOccurred"] = "{heir} succeeded {predecessor} at {@place}.",
        ["TheftFoiled"] = "{victim} caught {thief} reaching for {artifact}.",
        ["WarDeclared"] = "{attacker} declared war on {defender} for {=casus belli}: {war}.",
    };

    private readonly Func<EntityRef, string?> _nameOf = Names.Of(world);

    public string Narrate(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var sentence = Templates.TryGetValue(ev.Type, out var template) && Fill(template, ev) is { } filled
            ? template.StartsWith('{') ? filled : LowerFirst(filled)
            : LowerFirst(Generic(ev));
        var text = new StringBuilder($"In year {ev.Date.Year}, {sentence}");
        if (ev.Payload.TryGetValue("motive", out var motive)) text.Append($" ({motive})");
        if (ev.Payload.TryGetValue("account", out var account)) text.Append($" {account}");
        return text.ToString();
    }

    public string NarrateChain(ChainNode chain)
    {
        var text = new StringBuilder(Narrate(chain.Event));
        Causes(text, chain, first: true);
        return text.ToString();
    }

    private void Causes(StringBuilder text, ChainNode node, bool first)
    {
        foreach (var cause in node.Causes)
        {
            var connective = first ? "This happened because" : "That followed from what came before:";
            text.Append($" {connective} {LowerFirst(Narrate(cause.Event))}");
            Causes(text, cause, first: false);
        }
    }

    private string? Fill(string template, WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var missing = false;
        var result = Placeholder().Replace(template, m =>
        {
            var key = m.Groups[1].Value;
            string? value = key switch
            {
                "@place" => ev.Place is { } place ? _nameOf(place) : null,
                _ when key.StartsWith('=') => ev.Payload.GetValueOrDefault(key[1..]),
                _ => ev.Actors.TryGetValue(key, out var actor) ? _nameOf(actor) : null,
            };
            missing |= value is null;
            return value ?? "";
        });
        return missing ? null : result;
    }

    private string Generic(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        var who = ev.Actors.Select(a => _nameOf(a.Value)).Where(n => n is not null).ToList();
        var text = Briefs.Spaced(ev.Type);
        if (who.Count > 0) text += $" involving {string.Join(" and ", who)}";
        if (ev.Place is { } place && _nameOf(place) is { } where) text += $" at {where}";
        return char.ToUpperInvariant(text[0]) + text[1..] + ".";
    }

    /// <summary>Sentences that open with a name keep their capital; others continue the "In year N," clause.</summary>
    private static string LowerFirst(string sentence) =>
        sentence.Length > 0 ? char.ToLowerInvariant(sentence[0]) + sentence[1..] : sentence;

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\{([^}]+)\}")]
    private static partial Regex Placeholder();
}
```

### Task 8: Providers: Claude and OpenRouter

A new project, `FantasyWorld.Llm`, keeps the SDK out of the engine library. `AnthropicProvider` uses the official Anthropic C# SDK: model `claude-opus-5-5` by default, the world bible as a cached system block, structured outputs (`OutputConfig.Format` with the request's JSON schema), and effort low for story beats and medium for narration (Claude Opus 5.5 always thinks, so effort sets depth and the output budget gets headroom). A refusal stop reason (with its billed usage) and every SDK, network or timeout error become provider exceptions, so the gateway falls back; the client allows one retry and a minute per attempt so a story beat never stalls the game, and the provider is only built when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set. `OpenRouterProvider` calls OpenRouter's chat-completions API with a JSON-schema response format (bare Claude model names are routed as `anthropic/…`, with the same thinking headroom) and reads its reported cost. Credentials come from `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`, never from the world file.

**Files:**
- Create: `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/AnthropicProvider.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/FantasyWorld.Llm.csproj`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/LlmProviders.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/OpenRouterProvider.cs`, `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/Pricing.cs`
- Modify: `sim/FantasyWorld.sln`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Llm/ProviderTests.cs`
- Test (modify): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.csproj`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1.
- Produces: project `sim/FantasyWorld.Llm` (package `Anthropic` 12.53.0); `record Price(Input, Output, CacheRead, CacheWrite) { static For(model); Cost(input, output, cacheRead, cacheWrite) }` (USD per million tokens; unknown models priced at the top tier); `AnthropicProvider(AnthropicClient? client = null) { ThinkingHeadroom = 8000; static DefaultClient(); static BuildParams(request, model) }`; `OpenRouterProvider(apiKey, HttpClient? http = null) { Endpoint; static Route(model); static BuildBody(request, model) }`; `static class LlmProviders { Create(settings, env?) }`.

```csharp
using System.Net;
using System.Text.Json;
using FantasyWorld.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Llm;

public class ProviderTests
{
    private sealed class Handler(HttpStatusCode status, string body) : HttpMessageHandler
    {
        public HttpRequestMessage? Request { get; private set; }
        public string? RequestBody { get; private set; }

        protected override async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(HttpRequestMessage request, CancellationToken ct)
        {
            Request = request;
            RequestBody = await request.Content!.ReadAsStringAsync(ct);
            return new HttpResponseMessage(status) { Content = new StringContent(body) };
        }
    }

    private static readonly LlmRequest Decide = new(ModelTier.Decide, "k", "THE BIBLE", "Choose.",
        "{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{\"choice\":{\"type\":\"string\"}},\"required\":[\"choice\"],\"additionalProperties\":false}", 512);

    [Fact]
    public void AnthropicRequests_CacheTheBible_AndAskForSchemaJson()
    {
        var p = AnthropicProvider.BuildParams(Decide, "claude-opus-5-5");
        Assert.Equal(512 + AnthropicProvider.ThinkingHeadroom, p.MaxTokens);
        var json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(p);
        Assert.Contains("\"model\":\"claude-opus-5-5\"", json);
        Assert.Contains("\"cache_control\":{\"type\":\"ephemeral\"", json);
        Assert.Contains("\"effort\":\"low\"", json);
        Assert.Contains("\"type\":\"json_schema\"", json);
        Assert.Contains("THE BIBLE", json);
        Assert.Contains("\"effort\":\"medium\"", JsonSerializer.Serialize(AnthropicProvider.BuildParams(Decide with { Tier = ModelTier.Narrate }, "claude-opus-5-5")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OpenRouter_SendsSchemaJson_AndReadsCost()
    {
        var handler = new Handler(HttpStatusCode.OK,
            "{\"choices\":[{\"finish_reason\":\"stop\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"{\\\"choice\\\":\\\"a\\\"}\"}}],\"usage\":{\"prompt_tokens\":900,\"completion_tokens\":40,\"cost\":0.0021}}");
        var provider = new OpenRouterProvider("sk-test", new HttpClient(handler));

        var response = await provider.CompleteJsonAsync(Decide, "anthropic/claude-opus-5-5");

        Assert.Equal("{\"choice\":\"a\"}", response.Json);
        Assert.Equal((900, 40, 0.0021m), (response.InputTokens, response.OutputTokens, response.CostUsd));
        Assert.Equal("Bearer sk-test", handler.Request!.Headers.Authorization!.ToString());
        var body = JsonDocument.Parse(handler.RequestBody!).RootElement;
        Assert.Equal("json_schema", body.GetProperty("response_format").GetProperty("type").GetString());
        Assert.Equal("THE BIBLE", body.GetProperty("messages")[0].GetProperty("content").GetString());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OpenRouterErrors_AreUnavailability()
    {
        var provider = new OpenRouterProvider("sk-test", new HttpClient(new Handler(HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests, "{}")));
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<LlmUnavailableException>(() => provider.CompleteJsonAsync(Decide, "m"));
        var garbled = new OpenRouterProvider("sk-test", new HttpClient(new Handler(HttpStatusCode.OK, "not json")));
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<LlmUnavailableException>(() => garbled.CompleteJsonAsync(Decide, "m"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Prices_CoverTheLedger_AndUnknownModelsErrHigh()
    {
        Assert.Equal(0.0042m, Price.For("claude-opus-5-5").Cost(1000, 0, 0, 0) + Price.For("claude-opus-5-5").Cost(0, 0, 1000, 0));
        Assert.Equal(Price.For("claude-opus-5-5"), Price.For("anthropic/claude-opus-5-5"));
        Assert.True(Price.For("mystery-model").Output >= Price.For("claude-opus-5-5").Output);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Providers_FollowTheSettings_AndNeedCredentials()
    {
        Assert.Null(LlmProviders.Create(new LlmSettings()));
        Assert.Null(LlmProviders.Create(new LlmSettings { Provider = "anthropic" }, _ => null));
        Assert.IsType<AnthropicProvider>(LlmProviders.Create(new LlmSettings { Provider = "anthropic" }, v => v == "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" ? "sk" : null));
        Assert.Null(LlmProviders.Create(new LlmSettings { Provider = "openrouter" }, _ => null));
        Assert.IsType<OpenRouterProvider>(LlmProviders.Create(new LlmSettings { Provider = "openrouter" }, _ => "sk"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OpenRouter_RoutesClaudeModels_LeavesThinkingRoom_AndReportsRefusals()
    {
        Assert.Equal("anthropic/claude-opus-5-5", OpenRouterProvider.Route("claude-opus-5-5"));
        Assert.Equal("openai/x", OpenRouterProvider.Route("openai/x"));
        var body = OpenRouterProvider.BuildBody(Decide, "claude-opus-5-5");
        Assert.Equal(512 + AnthropicProvider.ThinkingHeadroom, (int)body["max_tokens"]!);

        var filtered = new OpenRouterProvider("sk", new HttpClient(new Handler(HttpStatusCode.OK,
            "{\"choices\":[{\"finish_reason\":\"content_filter\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"\"}}],\"usage\":{\"prompt_tokens\":9,\"completion_tokens\":0,\"cost\":0.001}}")));
        var refused = await Assert.ThrowsAsync<LlmRefusedException>(() => filtered.CompleteJsonAsync(Decide, "claude-opus-5-5"));
        Assert.Equal(0.001m, refused.Response!.CostUsd);
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/AnthropicProvider.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using Anthropic;
using Anthropic.Models.Messages;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;

namespace FantasyWorld.Llm;

/// <summary>
/// Claude through the official Anthropic C# SDK (spec §8.5). Credentials are resolved by the SDK from the
/// <c>ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</c> environment variable. The world bible is the cached system prefix; answers use
/// structured outputs (JSON schema). Thinking is always on for Claude Opus 5.5, so effort sets the depth:
/// low for story beats, medium for narration.
/// </summary>
public sealed class AnthropicProvider(AnthropicClient? client = null) : ILlmProvider
{
    /// <summary>Thinking shares the output budget, so the answer's own budget gets generous headroom.</summary>
    public const int ThinkingHeadroom = 8000;

    private AnthropicClient? _client = client;

    public string Name => "anthropic";

    public static MessageCreateParams BuildParams(LlmRequest request, string model) => new()
    {
        Model = model,
        MaxTokens = request.MaxTokens + ThinkingHeadroom,
        System = new List<TextBlockParam> { new() { Text = request.System, CacheControl = new CacheControlEphemeral() } },
        OutputConfig = new OutputConfig
        {
            Effort = request.Tier == ModelTier.Narrate ? Effort.Medium : Effort.Low,
            Format = new JsonOutputFormat { Schema = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, JsonElement>>(request.Schema)! },
        },
        Messages = [new() { Role = Role.User, Content = request.Prompt }],
    };

    /// <summary>A game must not stall on one story beat: one retry, a minute at most per attempt.</summary>
    public static AnthropicClient DefaultClient() => new() { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60), MaxRetries = 1 };

    public async Task<LlmResponse> CompleteJsonAsync(LlmRequest request, string model, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        LlmResponse response;
        bool refused;
        try
        {
            _client ??= DefaultClient();
            var message = await _client.Messages.Create(BuildParams(request, model), ct);
            refused = message.StopReason == StopReason.Refusal;
            var json = string.Concat(message.Content.Select(b => b.Value).OfType<TextBlock>().Select(t => t.Text));
            var usage = message.Usage;
            var (cacheRead, cacheWrite) = (usage.CacheReadInputTokens ?? 0, usage.CacheCreationInputTokens ?? 0);
            response = new LlmResponse(json, Name, model, usage.InputTokens, usage.OutputTokens, cacheRead, cacheWrite,
                Price.For(model).Cost(usage.InputTokens, usage.OutputTokens, cacheRead, cacheWrite));
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            // Network, auth, timeouts, rate limits, malformed responses: all mean "not now".
            throw new LlmUnavailableException($"Claude is unavailable: {e.Message}", e);
        }
        if (refused) throw new LlmRefusedException("Claude declined this request.", response);
        return response;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;

namespace FantasyWorld.Llm;

/// <summary>Builds the provider a world's settings name, or null (offline) when its credentials are not set.</summary>
public static class LlmProviders
{
    public static ILlmProvider? Create(LlmSettings settings, Func<string, string?>? env = null)
    {
        env ??= Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable;
        return settings.Provider switch
        {
            "anthropic" when env("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY") is { Length: > 0 } => new AnthropicProvider(),
            "openrouter" when env("OPENROUTER_API_KEY") is { Length: > 0 } key => new OpenRouterProvider(key),
            _ => null,
        };
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/OpenRouterProvider.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;

namespace FantasyWorld.Llm;

/// <summary>
/// Any model behind OpenRouter's chat-completions HTTP API (spec §8.5), with JSON-schema structured output.
/// The key comes from <c>OPENROUTER_API_KEY</c>. Cost is OpenRouter's reported cost when given, else the price table.
/// </summary>
public sealed class OpenRouterProvider(string apiKey, HttpClient? http = null) : ILlmProvider
{
    public const string Endpoint = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions";

    private readonly HttpClient _http = http ?? new HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2) };

    public string Name => "openrouter";

    /// <summary>OpenRouter names models by vendor; a bare Claude model name means Anthropic's.</summary>
    public static string Route(string model) => model.Contains('/', StringComparison.Ordinal) ? model : "anthropic/" + model;

    public static JsonObject BuildBody(LlmRequest request, string model) => new()
    {
        ["model"] = Route(model),
        ["max_tokens"] = request.MaxTokens + AnthropicProvider.ThinkingHeadroom,
        ["messages"] = new JsonArray
        {
            new JsonObject { ["role"] = "system", ["content"] = request.System },
            new JsonObject { ["role"] = "user", ["content"] = request.Prompt },
        },
        ["response_format"] = new JsonObject
        {
            ["type"] = "json_schema",
            ["json_schema"] = new JsonObject { ["name"] = request.Tier.ToString().ToLowerInvariant(), ["strict"] = true, ["schema"] = JsonNode.Parse(request.Schema) },
        },
        ["usage"] = new JsonObject { ["include"] = true },
    };

    public async Task<LlmResponse> CompleteJsonAsync(LlmRequest request, string model, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        using var message = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, Endpoint)
        {
            Content = new StringContent(BuildBody(request, model).ToJsonString(), Encoding.UTF8, "application/json"),
        };
        message.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", apiKey);
        try
        {
            using var response = await _http.SendAsync(message, ct);
            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(ct);
            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode) throw new LlmUnavailableException($"OpenRouter answered {(int)response.StatusCode}.");
            using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(body);
            var root = doc.RootElement;
            var choice = root.GetProperty("choices")[0];
            var json = choice.GetProperty("message").GetProperty("content").GetString() ?? "";
            var usage = root.TryGetProperty("usage", out var u) ? u : default;
            long Tokens(string name) => usage.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object && usage.TryGetProperty(name, out var v) ? v.GetInt64() : 0;
            var (input, output) = (Tokens("prompt_tokens"), Tokens("completion_tokens"));
            var cost = usage.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Object && usage.TryGetProperty("cost", out var c) && c.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Number
                ? c.GetDecimal()
                : Price.For(model).Cost(input, output, 0, 0);
            var result = new LlmResponse(json, Name, Route(model), input, output, 0, 0, cost);
            if (choice.TryGetProperty("finish_reason", out var finish) && finish.GetString() is "content_filter")
                throw new LlmRefusedException("The model declined this request.", result);
            return result;
        }
        catch (LlmUnavailableException)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            throw new LlmUnavailableException($"OpenRouter is unavailable: {e.Message}", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Llm/Pricing.cs`:
```csharp
namespace FantasyWorld.Llm;

/// <summary>USD per million tokens, for the cost ledger. Unknown models are priced at the top tier so the spend cap errs safe.</summary>
public sealed record Price(decimal Input, decimal Output, decimal CacheRead, decimal CacheWrite)
{
    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, Price> Known = new Dictionary<string, Price>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
    {
        ["claude-opus-5-5"] = new(4m, 20m, 0.20m, 5m),
        ["claude-sonnet-5-5"] = new(2m, 10m, 0.20m, 2.5m),
        ["claude-haiku-4-5"] = new(1m, 5m, 0.10m, 1.25m),
        ["claude-fable-5-1"] = new(10m, 50m, 0.25m, 12.5m),
    };

    public static Price For(string model) =>
        Known.TryGetValue(model.StartsWith("anthropic/", StringComparison.Ordinal) ? model["anthropic/".Length..] : model, out var price)
            ? price
            : Known["claude-fable-5-1"];

    public decimal Cost(long input, long output, long cacheRead, long cacheWrite) =>
        (input * Input + output * Output + cacheRead * CacheRead + cacheWrite * CacheWrite) / 1_000_000m;
}
```

### Task 9: LLM in the CLI

`fw advance` and `fw catchup` now load the world's journal and settings and run every system with the LLM decider, arbiter and inventor; offline they replay journaled answers and otherwise use the rules, drawing the same randomness, so offline worlds are unchanged. The month's answers are saved with its events. New commands: `fw llm` shows and sets provider, models, budgets and cap; `fw tell <event>` narrates a why-chain (templates offline); `fw stats` summarises the world and LLM spend. `fw artifact` shows lore.

```csharp
using System.Text.Json;
using FantasyWorld.Cli;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Support;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Cli;

public class CliLlmTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset T0 = new(2026, 10, 5, 23, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);

    /// <summary>Answers every job with the first legal key, a name or a sentence.</summary>
    public static FakeLlmProvider Agreeable() => new(request =>
    {
        var properties = JsonDocument.Parse(request.Schema).RootElement.GetProperty("properties");
        string First(string field) => properties.GetProperty(field).GetProperty("enum")[0].GetString()!;
        if (properties.TryGetProperty("choice", out _)) return $"{{\"choice\":\"{First("choice")}\",\"reasoning\":\"It seemed right.\",\"flavor\":\"\"}}";
        if (properties.TryGetProperty("outcome", out _)) return $"{{\"outcome\":\"{First("outcome")}\",\"turning_point\":\"\",\"account\":\"So it went.\"}}";
        if (properties.TryGetProperty("name", out _)) return "{\"name\":\"The Named Thing\",\"lore\":\"Remembered in song.\"}";
        return "{\"text\":\"Once, long ago, it happened.\"}";
    });

    private static (int Code, string Output) Run(Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?>? providers, params string[] args)
    {

        return (code, output.ToString());
    }

    [Fact]

        Assert.Contains("motive=It seemed right.", text);
        var stats = Run(null, "stats", file.Path).Output;
        Assert.Contains($"LLM: {fake.Requests.Count} answers journaled", stats);

}
```

`sim/FantasyWorld.Cli/CliApp.Llm.cs`:
```csharp
using System.Globalization;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Narration;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;

namespace FantasyWorld.Cli;

public static partial class CliApp
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Loads a world with its LLM journal and settings, and the LLM decider, arbiter and inventor behind every
    /// system. Offline (provider none, or no credentials) they replay journaled answers and otherwise use the rules.
    /// </summary>
    private static (Simulator Sim, LlmGateway Gateway) LoadLive(WorldStore store, Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?> providers)
    {
        var (world, log, queue) = store.Load();
        var settings = store.LoadLlmSettings();
        var gateway = new LlmGateway(settings.Enabled ? providers(settings) : null, settings, store.LoadJournal());
        var story = new StoryContext(world, log, gateway);
        var systems = DefaultSystems.Create(new LlmDecider(story), new LlmArbiter(story), new LlmInventor(story));
        return (new Simulator(world, log, queue, systems), gateway);
    }

    private static void PrintLlmSummary(TextWriter output, LlmGateway gateway, IReadOnlyList<LlmCall> fresh)
    {
        if (!gateway.Settings.Enabled && gateway.Replayed == 0) return;
        var spend = fresh.Sum(c => c.CostUsd);
        output.WriteLine($"LLM: {fresh.Count} new answer(s) (${spend.ToString("0.0000", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}), "
                         + $"{gateway.Replayed} replayed, {gateway.Fallbacks} by the rules{(gateway.Online ? "" : " (offline)")}.");
        if (gateway.Failure is { } failure) output.WriteLine($"LLM provider unavailable, continued offline: {failure}");
    }

    private static int Tell(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output, Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?> providers)
    {
        if (rest.Length < 1) return UsageError(output);
        var eventId = long.Parse(rest[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        var depth = int.Parse(Option(rest, "--depth") ?? "4", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (world, log, _) = store.Load();
        if (!log.TryGet(eventId, out _)) throw new KeyNotFoundException($"No event #{eventId}.");
        var settings = store.LoadLlmSettings();
        var gateway = new LlmGateway(settings.Enabled ? providers(settings) : null, settings, store.LoadJournal());
        output.WriteLine(new LlmNarrator(new StoryContext(world, log, gateway)).NarrateChain(new HistoryQuery(log).WhyChain(eventId, depth)));
        store.SaveJournal(gateway.Journal);
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Stats(string path, TextWriter output, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock)
    {
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (world, log, _) = store.Load();
        var journal = store.LoadJournal();
        output.WriteLine($"{world.Date}: {log.Count} events, {world.ActiveCharacters.Count()} living notables, "
                         + $"{world.Realms.Values.Count(r => !r.Destroyed && r.LiegeId == 0)} independent realms, "
                         + $"{world.Wars.Values.Count(w => !w.Ended)} wars under way, {world.Artifacts.Count} artifacts.");
        var monthAgo = clock().AddDays(-30);
        output.WriteLine($"LLM: {journal.Count} answers journaled ({string.Join(", ", journal.All.GroupBy(c => c.Tier).OrderBy(g => g.Key).Select(g => $"{g.Key.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()} {g.Count()}"))}); "
                         + $"spend ${journal.All.Sum(c => c.CostUsd).ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} in all, "
                         + $"${journal.SpendSince(monthAgo).ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} in the last 30 days.");
        return 0;
    }

    private static int LlmCommand(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var settings = store.LoadLlmSettings();
        var models = new SortedDictionary<string, string>(settings.Models, StringComparer.Ordinal);
        for (var i = 0; i < rest.Length - 1; i++)
        {
            if (rest[i] != "--model") continue;
            var parts = rest[i + 1].Split('=', 2);
            if (parts.Length != 2 || !Enum.GetNames<ModelTier>().Contains(parts[0], StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || parts[1].Length == 0)
                throw new FormatException($"Use --model <tier>=<model>, with tier decide, arbitrate, invent or narrate (got '{rest[i + 1]}').");
            models[parts[0].ToLowerInvariant()] = parts[1];
        }
        var provider = Option(rest, "--provider") ?? settings.Provider;
        if (provider is not ("none" or "anthropic" or "openrouter"))
            throw new FormatException($"Unknown provider '{provider}'. Use none, anthropic or openrouter.");
        settings = settings with
        {
            Provider = provider,
            Models = models,
            StoryBeatsPerDay = int.Parse(Option(rest, "--story-beats") ?? settings.StoryBeatsPerDay.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            NarrationsPerDay = int.Parse(Option(rest, "--narrations") ?? settings.NarrationsPerDay.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            MonthlySpendCapUsd = decimal.Parse(Option(rest, "--cap") ?? settings.MonthlySpendCapUsd.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
        };
        if (settings.StoryBeatsPerDay < 0 || settings.NarrationsPerDay < 0 || settings.MonthlySpendCapUsd < 0)
            throw new FormatException("Budgets and the spend cap cannot be negative.");
        if (rest.Length > 0) store.SaveLlmSettings(settings);

        output.WriteLine($"provider: {settings.Provider}");
        foreach (var tier in Enum.GetValues<ModelTier>())
            output.WriteLine($"  {tier.ToString().ToLowerInvariant(),-9} {settings.ModelFor(tier)}");
        output.WriteLine($"budget: {settings.StoryBeatsPerDay} story beats and {settings.NarrationsPerDay} narrations per day; "
                         + $"cap ${settings.MonthlySpendCapUsd.ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} per 30 days");
        var variable = settings.Provider switch { "anthropic" => "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "openrouter" => "OPENROUTER_API_KEY", _ => null };
        if (variable is not null)
            output.WriteLine($"credentials: {variable} {(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(variable) is { Length: > 0 } ? "is set" : "is not set, so the game runs offline")}");
        return 0;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System.Globalization;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;

namespace FantasyWorld.Cli;

/// <summary>Commands about people and artifacts: characters, life, artifacts, artifact.</summary>
public static partial class CliApp
{
    private static readonly CultureInfo Inv = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;

    private static int Characters(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        var settlementText = Option(rest, "--settlement");
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var world = store.Load().World;
        var people = world.ActiveCharacters;
        if (settlementText is not null)
        {
            var id = long.Parse(settlementText, Inv);
            if (!world.Settlements.ContainsKey(id)) throw new KeyNotFoundException($"No settlement #{id}.");
            people = people.Where(c => c.SettlementId == id);
        }
        output.WriteLine("    id name                           age role        renown ambition   home");
        foreach (var c in people)
            output.WriteLine(string.Create(Inv,
                $"{c.Id,6} {FullName(world, c),-30} {c.Age(world.Date),3} {c.Role,-10} {c.Renown,6:0} {c.Ambition,-10} {world.Settlements[c.SettlementId].Name}"));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Life(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        if (rest.Length < 1) return UsageError(output);
        var id = long.Parse(rest[0], Inv);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (world, log, _) = store.Load();
        var c = world.Characters.TryGetValue(id, out var found) ? found : throw new KeyNotFoundException($"No character #{id}.");
        string Name(long who) => who != 0 && world.Characters.TryGetValue(who, out var k) ? $"{FullName(world, k)} (char#{who})" : "—";

        var born = new Sim.Core.WorldDate(c.BirthDay);
        var status = c.Died is { } died ? $"died {died} aged {c.Age(died)} of {c.DeathCause}" : $"aged {c.Age(world.Date)}";
        output.WriteLine($"{FullName(world, c)} (char#{c.Id}) — born {born}, {status}");
        output.WriteLine($"  {c.Sex}, {world.Cultures.GetValueOrDefault(c.CultureId)?.Name ?? "?"}, of {world.Faiths.GetValueOrDefault(c.FaithId)?.Name ?? "?"}; " +
                         $"{c.Role} at {world.Settlements[c.SettlementId].Name}{(c.Faded ? " (faded into the populace)" : "")}");
        output.WriteLine($"  traits: {string.Join(", ", c.Traits)}; ambition: {c.Ambition}; renown {c.Renown.ToString("0", Inv)}");
        var a = c.Attributes;
        output.WriteLine($"  martial {a.Martial}, diplomacy {a.Diplomacy}, stewardship {a.Stewardship}, intrigue {a.Intrigue}, lore {a.Lore}, prowess {a.Prowess}");
        if (c.Wounds.Count > 0) output.WriteLine($"  wounds: {string.Join(", ", c.Wounds)}");
        output.WriteLine($"  parents: {Name(c.FatherId)}, {Name(c.MotherId)}; spouse: {Name(c.SpouseId)}");
        if (c.Children.Count > 0) output.WriteLine($"  children: {string.Join(", ", c.Children.Select(Name))}");
        var held = world.Artifacts.Values.Where(x => x.Place == ArtifactPlace.Held && x.HolderId == c.Id).ToList();
        if (held.Count > 0) output.WriteLine($"  carries: {string.Join(", ", held.Select(x => $"{x.Name} (artifact#{x.Id})"))}");
        var feelings = c.Memories.Where(m => m.About != 0).GroupBy(m => m.About)
            .Select(g => (Who: g.Key, Opinion: Relations.Opinion(c, world.Characters[g.Key])))
            .Where(f => Math.Abs(f.Opinion) >= Relations.FriendAt).OrderBy(f => f.Who).ToList();
        if (feelings.Count > 0)
            output.WriteLine("  bonds: " + string.Join(", ", feelings.Select(f => $"{(f.Opinion > 0 ? "friend" : "rival")} {Name(f.Who)}")));
        output.WriteLine("  Life:");
        foreach (var ev in new HistoryQuery(log).Life(c.Ref)) output.WriteLine("    " + EventText.Describe(ev, NameOf(world)));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int ArtifactList(string path, TextWriter output)
    {
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var world = store.Load().World;
        output.WriteLine("    id name                                type     material     fame  where");
        foreach (var x in world.Artifacts.Values)
            output.WriteLine(string.Create(Inv, $"{x.Id,6} {x.Name,-35} {x.Type,-8} {x.Material,-12} {x.Fame,4:0}  {Whereabouts(world, x)}"));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int ArtifactDetail(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        if (rest.Length < 1) return UsageError(output);
        var id = long.Parse(rest[0], Inv);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (world, log, _) = store.Load();
        var x = world.Artifacts.TryGetValue(id, out var found) ? found : throw new KeyNotFoundException($"No artifact #{id}.");
        output.WriteLine($"{x.Name} (artifact#{x.Id}) — {x.Type.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()} of {x.Material}, fame {x.Fame.ToString("0", Inv)}");
        output.WriteLine($"  {Whereabouts(world, x)}");
        output.WriteLine("  powers: " + string.Join(", ", x.Powers.Select(p => p.Kind == "attribute" ? $"+{p.Amount} {p.Attribute}" : p.Kind)));
        if (x.CurseRevealed) output.WriteLine($"  cursed: {x.Curse}");
        if (x.Lore.Length > 0) output.WriteLine($"  lore: {x.Lore}");
        output.WriteLine(x.OriginEventId == 0 ? "  Made before recorded history." : "  Made: " + EventText.Describe(log.Get(x.OriginEventId), NameOf(world)));
        if (x.Provenance.Count > 0)
        {
            output.WriteLine("  Provenance:");
            foreach (var entry in x.Provenance)
                output.WriteLine($"    {entry.How}: {EventText.Describe(log.Get(entry.EventId), NameOf(world))}");
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static string Whereabouts(World world, Artifact x) => x.Place switch
    {
        ArtifactPlace.Held => $"held by {FullName(world, world.Characters[x.HolderId])} (char#{x.HolderId})",
        ArtifactPlace.Treasury => $"in the treasury of {world.Settlements[x.SettlementId].Name}",
        ArtifactPlace.Lost => $"lost in {world.Regions[x.RegionId].Name}",
        _ => "destroyed",
    };

    private static string FullName(World world, Character c) => Names.Full(world, c);
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Cli/CliApp.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Data.Common;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.Json;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Query;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Cli;

public static partial class CliApp
{
    public const string Usage = """
        Usage:
          fw new <world-file> [--seed <n>] [--size tiny|medium|large]
          fw advance <world-file> <duration>        e.g. 30d, 4mo, 7y (debug fast-forward)
          fw catchup <world-file>                   simulate the real time that has passed
          fw log <world-file> [--since <event-id>]
          fw why <world-file> <event-id> [--depth <n>]
          fw map <world-file> [--layer biome|regions|realms]
          fw regions <world-file>
          fw region <world-file> <region-id>
          fw characters <world-file> [--settlement <id>]
          fw life <world-file> <character-id>
          fw artifacts <world-file>
          fw artifact <world-file> <artifact-id>
          fw realms <world-file>
          fw realm <world-file> <realm-id>
          fw wars <world-file>
          fw war <world-file> <war-id>
          fw tell <world-file> <event-id> [--depth <n>]   the why-chain as prose
          fw stats <world-file>
          fw llm <world-file> [--provider none|anthropic|openrouter] [--model <tier>=<model>]...
                 [--story-beats <per-day>] [--narrations <per-day>] [--cap <usd-per-month>]
        """;

    /// <summary>Runs one command. Providers builds the LLM backend a world's settings name (tests inject fakes).</summary>
    public static int Run(string[] args, TextWriter output, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock, Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?>? providers = null)
    {
        providers ??= settings => LlmProviders.Create(settings);
        if (args.Length < 2)
            return UsageError(output);

        var path = args[1];
        var rest = args.Skip(2).ToArray();
        try
        {
            return args[0] switch
            {
                "new" => New(path, rest, output, clock),
                "advance" => Advance(path, rest, output, providers),
                "catchup" => CatchUpCommand(path, output, clock, providers),
                "log" => Log(path, rest, output),
                "why" => Why(path, rest, output),
                "map" => Map(path, rest, output),
                "regions" => Regions(path, output),
                "region" => RegionDetail(path, rest, output),
                "characters" => Characters(path, rest, output),
                "life" => Life(path, rest, output),
                "artifacts" => ArtifactList(path, output),
                "artifact" => ArtifactDetail(path, rest, output),
                "realms" => RealmList(path, output),
                "realm" => RealmDetail(path, rest, output),
                "wars" => WarList(path, output),
                "war" => WarDetail(path, rest, output),
                "tell" => Tell(path, rest, output, providers),
                "stats" => Stats(path, output, clock),
                "llm" => LlmCommand(path, rest, output),
                _ => UsageError(output),
            };
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is FormatException or ArgumentException or InvalidOperationException
                                       or KeyNotFoundException or IOException or OverflowException
                                       or DbException or JsonException)
        {
            output.WriteLine($"error: {ex.Message}");
            return 1;
        }
    }

    private static int New(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock)
    {
        if (File.Exists(path))
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"{path} already exists.");
        var now = clock();
        var seedText = Option(rest, "--seed");
        var seed = seedText is null ? (ulong)now.UtcTicks : ulong.Parse(seedText, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        var sizeText = Option(rest, "--size") ?? "medium";
        if (!Enum.TryParse<WorldSize>(sizeText, ignoreCase: true, out var size) || !Enum.IsDefined(size))
            throw new FormatException($"Unknown size '{sizeText}'. Use tiny, medium or large.");

        var world = WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(seed, size), now);
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(path))!);
        using var store = WorldStore.Open(path);
        store.Save(world, new EventLog(), new EventQueue());
        output.WriteLine($"Created world at {path} (seed {seed}, {size.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()}: " +
                         $"{world.Regions.Count} regions, {world.Settlements.Count} settlements).");
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Advance(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output, Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?> providers)
    {
        if (rest.Length < 1) return UsageError(output);
        var days = DurationParser.ParseDays(rest[0]);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (sim, gateway) = LoadLive(store, providers);
        var lastId = sim.Log.NextId - 1;
        sim.AdvanceDays(days);
        var fresh = gateway.Journal.Pending.ToList();
        store.Save(sim.World, sim.Log, sim.Queue, gateway.Journal);
        output.WriteLine($"Advanced {days} days to {sim.World.Date}.");
        PrintLlmSummary(output, gateway, fresh);
        PrintEvents(output, sim.World, sim.Log.Since(lastId));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int CatchUpCommand(string path, TextWriter output, Func<DateTimeOffset> clock, Func<LlmSettings, ILlmProvider?> providers)
    {
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (sim, gateway) = LoadLive(store, providers);
        var lastId = sim.Log.NextId - 1;
        var result = CatchUp.Run(sim, clock());
        var fresh = gateway.Journal.Pending.ToList();
        store.Save(sim.World, sim.Log, sim.Queue, gateway.Journal);
        output.WriteLine($"Advanced {result.DaysAdvanced} days: {result.From} -> {result.To}.");
        if (result.DaysRemaining > 0)
            output.WriteLine($"The world is still {result.DaysRemaining} days behind; run catchup again later.");
        PrintLlmSummary(output, gateway, fresh);
        PrintEvents(output, sim.World, sim.Log.Since(lastId));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Log(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        var since = long.Parse(Option(rest, "--since") ?? "0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var sim = Load(store);
        PrintEvents(output, sim.World, sim.Log.Since(since));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Why(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        if (rest.Length < 1) return UsageError(output);
        var eventId = long.Parse(rest[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        var depth = int.Parse(Option(rest, "--depth") ?? "4", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var sim = Load(store);
        var chain = new HistoryQuery(sim.Log).WhyChain(eventId, depth);
        PrintChain(output, sim.World, chain, 0);
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Map(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        var layer = Option(rest, "--layer") ?? "biome";
        if (layer is not ("biome" or "regions" or "realms")) throw new FormatException($"Unknown layer '{layer}'. Use biome, regions or realms.");
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        output.WriteLine(MapRenderer.Render(store.Load().World, layer));
        return 0;
    }

    private static int Regions(string path, TextWriter output)
    {
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var world = store.Load().World;
        output.WriteLine("  id name               biome      climate      people  fert danger  ley   food  conditions");
        foreach (var r in world.Regions.Values)
        {
            var people = world.LivingSettlements(r).Sum(s => s.Population);
            output.WriteLine(string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                $"{r.Id,4} {r.Name,-18} {r.Biome,-10} {r.Climate,-10} {people,8} {r.Fertility,5:0} {r.Danger,6:0} {r.LeyPower,4:0} {FoodMonths(world, r),5:0.0}mo  {string.Join(",", r.Conditions.Keys)}"));
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static int RegionDetail(string path, string[] rest, TextWriter output)
    {
        if (rest.Length < 1) return UsageError(output);
        var id = long.Parse(rest[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        using var store = OpenExisting(path);
        var (world, log, _) = store.Load();
        var r = world.Regions.TryGetValue(id, out var found) ? found : throw new KeyNotFoundException($"No region #{id}.");

        var inv = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
        output.WriteLine($"{r.Name} (region#{r.Id}) — {r.Biome}, {r.Climate}{(r.Coastal ? ", coastal" : "")}{(r.HasRiver ? ", river" : "")}");
        output.WriteLine(string.Create(inv,
            $"  tiles {r.TileCount}, farmland {r.Farmland:0}, fertility {r.Fertility:0}/{r.BaseFertility:0}, danger {r.Danger:0}, ley {r.LeyPower:0}, food {FoodMonths(world, r):0.0} months"));
        if (world.Cultures.TryGetValue(r.CultureId, out var culture))
            output.WriteLine($"  culture {culture.Name}, faith {world.Faiths[r.FaithId].Name}");
        output.WriteLine("  resources: " + string.Join(", ", r.Resources.Select(p => string.Create(inv, $"{p.Key} {p.Value.Yield:0.#}/{p.Value.Capacity:0.#}"))));
        if (r.Conditions.Count > 0)
            output.WriteLine("  conditions: " + string.Join(", ", r.Conditions.Select(c => $"{c.Key} (#{c.Value})")));
        if (world.Geography is { } geography)
            output.WriteLine("  neighbours: " + string.Join(", ", geography.Graph.LinksOf(r.Id).Select(l =>
                string.Create(inv, $"{world.Regions[l.Other(r.Id)].Name} {l.TravelDays:0.#}d{(l.Kind == LinkKind.Sea ? " by sea" : "")}"))));
        output.WriteLine("  Settlements:");
        foreach (var s in r.Settlements.Select(sid => world.Settlements[sid]))
            output.WriteLine(string.Create(inv,
                $"    {s.Name} (settlement#{s.Id}) {(s.Abandoned ? "abandoned" : s.Tier.ToString())}{(world.Characters.TryGetValue(s.LordId, out var lord) ? $" of {FullName(world, lord)}" : "")}, {s.Population} people, unrest {s.Unrest:0}, wealth {s.Wealth:0}, {string.Join("/", s.Buildings)}"));
        if (r.Memory.Count > 0)
        {
            output.WriteLine("  Remembered for:");
            foreach (var ev in r.Memory.Select(log.Get)) output.WriteLine("    " + EventText.Describe(ev, NameOf(world)));
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static double FoodMonths(World world, Region region)
    {
        var need = world.LivingSettlements(region).Sum(Demography.Consumption);
        return need > 0 ? region.Food / need : 0;
    }

    private static WorldStore OpenExisting(string path)
    {
        if (!File.Exists(path))
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"No world file at {path}. Create one with: fw new {path}");
        return WorldStore.Open(path);
    }

    private static Simulator Load(WorldStore store)
    {
        var (world, log, queue) = store.Load();
        return new Simulator(world, log, queue, DefaultSystems.Create());
    }

    private static Func<EntityRef, string?> NameOf(World world) => Names.Of(world);

    private static void PrintEvents(TextWriter output, World world, IEnumerable<WorldEvent> events)
    {
        foreach (var ev in events) output.WriteLine(EventText.Describe(ev, NameOf(world)));
    }

    private static void PrintChain(TextWriter output, World world, ChainNode node, int indent)
    {
        var prefix = new string(' ', indent * 2) + (indent == 0 ? "" : "because ");
        output.WriteLine(prefix + EventText.Describe(node.Event, NameOf(world)));
        foreach (var cause in node.Causes) PrintChain(output, world, cause, indent + 1);
    }

    private static string? Option(string[] rest, string name)
    {
        var index = Array.IndexOf(rest, name);
        return index >= 0 && index + 1 < rest.Length ? rest[index + 1] : null;
    }

    private static int UsageError(TextWriter output)
    {
        output.WriteLine(Usage);
        return 2;
    }
}
```

Replace `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim/Persist/WorldStore.cs` entirely with:
```csharp
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.Json;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Model;
using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;

/// <summary>One SQLite file per world: snapshot, static geography (written once), the append-only event log and the LLM journal.</summary>
public sealed class WorldStore : IDisposable
{
    public const int CurrentSchemaVersion = 3;

    private const string Schema = """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshot (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1), json TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS geography (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1), json TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, day INTEGER NOT NULL, type TEXT NOT NULL, json TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_events_day ON events(day);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_events_type ON events(type);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_causes (
            cause_id INTEGER NOT NULL, effect_id INTEGER NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, weight REAL NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY (cause_id, effect_id));
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_actors (
            event_id INTEGER NOT NULL, role TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, entity_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY (event_id, role));
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_event_actors_entity ON event_actors(kind, entity_id);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS llm_calls (
            hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY, tier TEXT NOT NULL, day INTEGER NOT NULL, provider TEXT NOT NULL, model TEXT NOT NULL,
            response TEXT NOT NULL, input_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL, output_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL,
            cache_read_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL, cache_write_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL, cost_usd TEXT NOT NULL,
            real_utc TEXT NOT NULL, note TEXT NOT NULL);
        """;

    private readonly SqliteConnection _connection;
    private SqliteTransaction? _transaction;

    private WorldStore(SqliteConnection connection) => _connection = connection;

    public static WorldStore Open(string path)
    {
        var connectionString = new SqliteConnectionStringBuilder { DataSource = path, Pooling = false }.ToString();
        var connection = new SqliteConnection(connectionString);
        connection.Open();
        var store = new WorldStore(connection);
        store.Migrate();
        return store;
    }

    public int SchemaVersion => int.Parse(GetMeta("schema_version")!, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);

    public bool HasWorld => ScalarLong("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM snapshot") > 0;

    public long CountEvents() => ScalarLong("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events");

    public bool HasGeography => ScalarLong("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM geography") > 0;

    /// <summary>Saves the month in one transaction: new events, the snapshot and new LLM answers (spec §9).</summary>
    public void Save(World world, EventLog log, EventQueue queue, LlmJournal? journal = null)
    {
        _transaction = _connection.BeginTransaction();
        try
        {
            var persistedMax = ScalarLong("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id), 0) FROM events");
            foreach (var ev in log.Since(persistedMax)) InsertEvent(ev);

            if (world.Geography is { } geography && !HasGeography)
            {
                using var cmd = Command("INSERT INTO geography(id, json) VALUES (1, $json)");
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$json", JsonSerializer.Serialize(geography, SimJson.Options));
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }

            using (var cmd = Command("INSERT INTO snapshot(id, json) VALUES (1, $json) " +
                                     "ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET json = excluded.json"))
            {
                cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$json", JsonSerializer.Serialize(WorldSnapshot.Capture(world, queue), SimJson.Options));
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            }
            SetMeta("world_day", world.Date.Day.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            SetMeta("last_real_utc", world.LastRealUtc.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            foreach (var call in journal?.Pending ?? Array.Empty<LlmCall>()) InsertCall(call);

            _transaction.Commit();
            journal?.MarkSaved();
        }
        catch
        {
            _transaction.Rollback();
            throw;
        }
        finally
        {
            _transaction.Dispose();
            _transaction = null;
        }
    }

    public (World World, EventLog Log, EventQueue Queue) Load()
    {
        string json;
        using (var cmd = Command("SELECT json FROM snapshot WHERE id = 1"))
            json = cmd.ExecuteScalar() as string ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No world has been saved in this file.");
        var snapshot = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<WorldSnapshot>(json, SimJson.Options)
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("World snapshot is empty.");

        var log = new EventLog();
        using (var cmd = Command("SELECT json FROM events ORDER BY id"))
        using (var reader = cmd.ExecuteReader())
            while (reader.Read())
                log.Restore(JsonSerializer.Deserialize<WorldEvent>(reader.GetString(0), SimJson.Options)!);

        var world = snapshot.ToWorld();
        using (var cmd = Command("SELECT json FROM geography WHERE id = 1"))
        {
            if (cmd.ExecuteScalar() is string geography)
                world.Geography = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Geography>(geography, SimJson.Options);
        }

        return (world, log, snapshot.ToQueue());
    }

    /// <summary>Saves new LLM answers alone (narration, which changes nothing in the world).</summary>
    public void SaveJournal(LlmJournal journal)
    {
        _transaction = _connection.BeginTransaction();
        try
        {
            foreach (var call in journal.Pending) InsertCall(call);
            _transaction.Commit();
            journal.MarkSaved();
        }
        finally
        {
            _transaction.Dispose();
            _transaction = null;
        }
    }

    public LlmJournal LoadJournal()
    {
        var journal = new LlmJournal();
        using var cmd = Command("SELECT hash, tier, day, provider, model, response, input_tokens, output_tokens, " +
                                "cache_read_tokens, cache_write_tokens, cost_usd, real_utc, note FROM llm_calls ORDER BY hash");
        using var reader = cmd.ExecuteReader();
        while (reader.Read())
            journal.Restore(new LlmCall(reader.GetString(0), Enum.Parse<ModelTier>(reader.GetString(1)), reader.GetInt64(2),
                reader.GetString(3), reader.GetString(4), reader.GetString(5), reader.GetInt64(6), reader.GetInt64(7),
                reader.GetInt64(8), reader.GetInt64(9), decimal.Parse(reader.GetString(10), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
                DateTimeOffset.Parse(reader.GetString(11), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), reader.GetString(12)));
        return journal;
    }

    public LlmSettings LoadLlmSettings() =>
        GetMeta("llm_settings") is { } json ? JsonSerializer.Deserialize<LlmSettings>(json, SimJson.Options)! : new LlmSettings();

    public void SaveLlmSettings(LlmSettings settings) => SetMeta("llm_settings", JsonSerializer.Serialize(settings, SimJson.Options));

    public void Dispose() => _connection.Dispose();

    private void Migrate()
    {
        using (var cmd = Command("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")) cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        using (var cmd = Command(Schema)) cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        var version = GetMeta("schema_version");
        var current = version is null ? 0 : int.Parse(version, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        if (current > CurrentSchemaVersion)
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                $"World file schema {version} is newer than this build supports ({CurrentSchemaVersion}).");
        // 1 → 2 adds the geography table and 2 → 3 the llm_calls table; the schema script above creates both.
        if (current < CurrentSchemaVersion)
            SetMeta("schema_version", CurrentSchemaVersion.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
    }

    private void InsertEvent(WorldEvent ev)
    {
        using (var cmd = Command("INSERT INTO events(id, day, type, json) VALUES ($id, $day, $type, $json)"))
        {
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$id", ev.Id);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$day", ev.Date.Day);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$type", ev.Type);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$json", JsonSerializer.Serialize(ev, SimJson.Options));
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
        foreach (var cause in ev.Causes)
        {
            using var cmd = Command("INSERT INTO event_causes(cause_id, effect_id, kind, weight) VALUES ($c, $e, $k, $w)");
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$c", cause.EventId);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$e", ev.Id);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$k", cause.Kind.ToString());
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$w", cause.Weight);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
        var actors = ev.Actors.ToList();
        if (ev.Place is { } place) actors.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, EntityRef>("@place", place));
        foreach (var (role, entity) in actors)
        {
            using var cmd = Command("INSERT INTO event_actors(event_id, role, kind, entity_id) VALUES ($e, $r, $k, $i)");
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$e", ev.Id);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$r", role);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$k", entity.Kind);
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$i", entity.Id);
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
    }

    private void InsertCall(LlmCall call)
    {
        using var cmd = Command("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO llm_calls VALUES ($h, $t, $d, $p, $m, $r, $i, $o, $cr, $cw, $c, $u, $n)");
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$h", call.Hash);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$t", call.Tier.ToString());
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$d", call.Day);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$p", call.Provider);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$m", call.Model);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$r", call.Json);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$i", call.InputTokens);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$o", call.OutputTokens);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$cr", call.CacheReadTokens);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$cw", call.CacheWriteTokens);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$c", call.CostUsd.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$u", call.RealUtc.ToString("O", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$n", call.Note);
        cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
    }

    private string? GetMeta(string key)
    {
        using var cmd = Command("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = $key");
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$key", key);
        return cmd.ExecuteScalar() as string;
    }

    private void SetMeta(string key, string value)
    {
        using var cmd = Command("INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES ($key, $value) " +
                                "ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value");
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$key", key);
        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("$value", value);
        cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
    }

    private long ScalarLong(string sql)
    {
        using var cmd = Command(sql);
        return Convert.ToInt64(cmd.ExecuteScalar(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    }

    private SqliteCommand Command(string sql)
    {
        var cmd = _connection.CreateCommand();
        cmd.CommandText = sql;
        cmd.Transaction = _transaction;
        return cmd;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Agency;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;

/// <summary>
/// The fixed system order for monthly and yearly ticks (spec §7: Aging → Economy → Regions →
/// Politics → Military → Characters → Structures): settlements, realms and diplomacy are politics,
/// then armies, then the character systems.
/// </summary>
public static class DefaultSystems
{
    /// <summary>
    /// All systems, sharing one decider, arbiter and inventor (the LLM ones in a live game, Plan 5); null means the
    /// rules implementations. Both paths draw the same randomness, so offline worlds are unchanged by the choice.
    /// </summary>
    public static IReadOnlyList<ISimSystem> Create(IDecider? decider = null, IArbiter? arbiter = null, IInventor? inventor = null) => new ISimSystem[]
    {
        new LifeSystem(),
        new EconomySystem(),
        new RegionSystem(),
        new SettlementSystem(),
        new RealmSystem(inventor),
        new DiplomacySystem(decider, inventor),
        new MilitarySystem(arbiter, decider),
        new FamilySystem(decider),
        new RelationsSystem(),
        new DeedsSystem(decider, arbiter, inventor),
        new ArtifactSystem(),
        new PromotionSystem(),
    };
}
```

### Task 10: Determinism with an LLM, and README

These checks hold the LLM layer to spec §7: offline LLM agency leaves the world byte-identical to the rules; a world advanced with a provider replays from its journal alone with zero provider calls; and an LLM world is identical whether advanced in one go or in chunks across a save and reload. The README explains how to turn Claude on, the defaults, budgets and credentials.

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`
- Test (create): `sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Engine/LlmDeterminismTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces: `LlmDeterminismTests`; README section "Story beats from Claude".

- [x] **Step 1: Write the checks**

`sim/FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests/Engine/LlmDeterminismTests.cs`:
```csharp
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Engine;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Events;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Llm;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Persist;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Systems;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Cli;
using FantasyWorld.Sim.WorldGen;

namespace FantasyWorld.Sim.Tests.Engine;

/// <summary>
/// Spec §7: World = f(seed, event log); LLM results are stored and replay never re-queries. These pin down that
/// the LLM layer keeps that promise.
/// </summary>
public class LlmDeterminismTests
{
    private static readonly LlmSettings On = new() { Provider = "fake", StoryBeatsPerDay = 100_000 };

    private static (Simulator Sim, LlmGateway Gateway) Make(ILlmProvider? provider, LlmJournal journal, bool invariants = false)
    {
        var world = WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(11, WorldSize.Tiny), DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var log = new EventLog();
        var gateway = new LlmGateway(provider, provider is null ? new LlmSettings() : On, journal);
        var story = new StoryContext(world, log, gateway);
        var systems = DefaultSystems.Create(new LlmDecider(story), new LlmArbiter(story), new LlmInventor(story));
        return (new Simulator(world, log, new EventQueue(), systems, invariants), gateway);
    }

    private static string Hash(Simulator sim) => WorldHasher.Hash(sim.World, sim.Log, sim.Queue);

    [Fact]
    public void OfflineLlmAgency_LeavesTheWorldExactlyAsTheRulesWouldHave()
    {
        var rules = new Simulator(WorldGenerator.Generate(new WorldGenOptions(11, WorldSize.Tiny), DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch),
            new EventLog(), new EventQueue(), DefaultSystems.Create());
        var (offline, _) = Make(null, new LlmJournal());
        rules.AdvanceDays(360 * 30);
        offline.AdvanceDays(360 * 30);
        Assert.Equal(Hash(rules), Hash(offline));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnLlmWorld_ReplaysFromItsJournal_WithoutAsking()
    {
        var fake = CliLlmTests.Agreeable();
        var journal = new LlmJournal();
        var (live, _) = Make(fake, journal, invariants: true);
        live.AdvanceDays(360 * 20);
        Assert.NotEmpty(fake.Requests);
        Assert.Contains(live.Log.All, e => e.Payload.ContainsKey("motive"));

        var (replay, gateway) = Make(null, journal);
        replay.AdvanceDays(360 * 20);
        Assert.Equal(Hash(live), Hash(replay));
        Assert.Equal(journal.Count, gateway.Replayed);
        Assert.Equal(0, gateway.Asked);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnLlmWorld_IsChunkAndReloadInvariant()
    {
        var oneGo = CliLlmTests.Agreeable();
        var (whole, _) = Make(oneGo, new LlmJournal());
        whole.AdvanceDays(360 * 12);

        using var file = new Support.TempFile();
        var journal = new LlmJournal();
        var (first, _) = Make(CliLlmTests.Agreeable(), journal);
        first.AdvanceDays(360 * 5);
        using (var store = WorldStore.Open(file.Path)) store.Save(first.World, first.Log, first.Queue, journal);

        using var reopened = WorldStore.Open(file.Path);
        var (world, log, queue) = reopened.Load();
        var gateway = new LlmGateway(CliLlmTests.Agreeable(), On, reopened.LoadJournal());
        var story = new StoryContext(world, log, gateway);
        var resumed = new Simulator(world, log, queue, DefaultSystems.Create(new LlmDecider(story), new LlmArbiter(story), new LlmInventor(story)));
        resumed.AdvanceDays(360 * 7);

        Assert.Equal(Hash(whole), Hash(resumed));
    }

    /// <summary>Always picks the least likely listed outcome or option: upsets galore, so the drama budget engages.</summary>
    private static FakeLlmProvider Contrarian() => new(request =>
    {
        var properties = System.Text.Json.JsonDocument.Parse(request.Schema).RootElement.GetProperty("properties");
        string Last(string field) => properties.GetProperty(field).GetProperty("enum").EnumerateArray().Last().GetString()!;
        if (properties.TryGetProperty("choice", out _)) return $"{{\"choice\":\"{Last("choice")}\",\"reasoning\":\"Against the odds.\",\"flavor\":\"\"}}";
        if (properties.TryGetProperty("outcome", out _)) return $"{{\"outcome\":\"{Last("outcome")}\",\"turning_point\":\"\",\"account\":\"\"}}";
        if (properties.TryGetProperty("name", out _)) return "{\"name\":\"Upset\",\"lore\":\"\"}";
        return "{\"text\":\"x\"}";
    });

    [Fact]
    public void UnderTheDramaBudget_AReplayStillMatches()
    {
        var journal = new LlmJournal();
        var (live, _) = Make(Contrarian(), journal);
        live.AdvanceDays(360 * 20);
        Assert.Contains(journal.All, c => c.Note == "upset");

        var (replay, gateway) = Make(null, journal);
        replay.AdvanceDays(360 * 20);
        Assert.Equal(Hash(live), Hash(replay));
        Assert.Equal(journal.All.Count(c => c.Json.Length > 0), gateway.Replayed);
    }
}
```

Replace `README.md` entirely with:
````markdown
# Fantasy World

A persistent fantasy world simulation — the world keeps moving while you sleep.
1 real day = 1 world year. You are the chronicler: come back each morning to see who rose, who fell, and *why*.

## Layout

- `sim/` — headless .NET 8 simulation core, tests and the `fw` CLI
- `docs/superpowers/specs/` — design specs
- `docs/superpowers/plans/` — implementation plans

## Run

```bash
dotnet test sim
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- new worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db            # medium world (100 regions)
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- map worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db            # ASCII map (--layer regions)
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- catchup worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db        # simulate real elapsed time
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- advance worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db 50y    # debug fast-forward
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- regions worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- region worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db 12
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- why worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db <event-id>
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- characters worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db       # living notables
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- life worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db <char-id>    # a biography
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- artifacts worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- artifact worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db <id>     # powers and provenance
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- realms worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db           # kingdoms and their vassals
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- realm worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db <id>        # laws, council, treaties, eras
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- wars worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- war worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db <id>          # casus belli and the course of the war
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- map worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db --layer realms
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- tell worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db <event-id>    # the why-chain as prose
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- stats worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db             # world summary and LLM spend
```

## Story beats from Claude

The world runs fully offline by default: rules make every choice and templates tell the story. To let Claude
decide notables' choices, arbitrate duels, hunts and delves, name forged artifacts, wars and eras, and narrate
with `fw tell`:

```bash
export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=...        # never stored in the world file
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- llm worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db --provider anthropic --cap 5
dotnet run --project sim/FantasyWorld.Cli -- advance worlds/aerenmoor.fw.db 2y
```

Defaults: `claude-opus-5-5` for every job (`--model narrate=<model>` changes one), 60 story beats and 200
narrations per rolling day, and a $10 cap per 30 days; past it, or without credentials, the rules take over.
`--provider openrouter` uses `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` instead. Every answer is journaled in the world file, so
reloading or replaying a world never asks again, and `fw why` shows the motives and accounts it gave.

`--size tiny` makes a 64×64 world with 12 regions for quick experiments.
````
